([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-posterior-coverage-assessment))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-power-analysis**: Use when planning an interindividual single cell RNA-seq differential expression or eQTL study and you need to choose sample size, cells per sample, and sequencing depth to maximize detection power under a fixed budget. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-power-analysis))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing**: Use when raw 10x scRNA-seq FASTQ files (with optional CITE-seq ADT and hashing data) must be converted to a filtered, clustered, cell-type-annotated expression matrix via a reproducible end-to-end workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing--f380976ee3c848e8))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing**: Use when droplet-based 10X Chromium scRNA-seq data (mouse aorta light-dark cycle, hepatocyte time course) must be loaded as anndata objects and subset to cell types for circadian phase inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing--53f127b954c701fa))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-and-clustering**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices (e.g., from liver transplant or SRA cohorts such as SRP313633) need normalization, clustering, and annotation into cell populations before downstream cell-cell communication or trajectory analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-and-clustering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (e.g., GEO datasets like GSE117872) must be cleaned prior to bulk-to-single-cell drug response prediction, and cells/genes failing QC thresholds must be removed without discarding heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-normalization**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq counts must be prepared as a log-normalized (genes x cells) matrix for full-dimensionality kernel methods (e.g. q-diffusion) that require no feature selection or dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-normalization))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-pseudobulk**: Aggregate verified raw single-cell counts by specimen and cell population with donor-aware readiness checks; use for pseudobulk preparation, not cell-level population inference or embeddings as counts. ([AbhignanVajjala/Bioinformatics-agent-skills](https://github.com/AbhignanVajjala/Bioinformatics-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/single-cell-rnaseq-pseudobulk))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-qc-filtering**: Use when raw scRNAseq FASTQ/output from a time-series (e.g., GSE223109 mouse dermis) must be QC-filtered, integrated across samples, clustered, and annotated into major cell types with marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-qc-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-quality-assessment**: Use when a 10X Genomics barcoded BAM file (with barcodes.tsv.gz from outs/filtered_feature_bc_matrix/) needs per-cell quality assessment based on gene body coverage skewness before downstream scRNA-seq analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-quality-assessment))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-quality-control**: Use when raw scRNA-seq counts (Seurat object, csv/rds/h5 counts matrix, or 10X folder) enter the scWizard workflow and low-quality cells must be filtered prior to annotation of TME cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-quality-control))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-read-alignment**: Use when scRNA-Seq FASTQs must be aligned to hg38/mm10 for downstream TE expression analysis with SoloTE or scTE, where multi-mapping reads across repetitive elements must be retained rather than discarded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-read-alignment))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-reference-loading**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq reference (e.g. 10x Chromium, GSE173778) and a spatial transcriptomics dataset (e.g. 10x Visium slides) must be ingested as the starting point of a SpatialDDLS deconvolution workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-reference-loading))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-reference-pretraining**: Use when building a reference-based cell-type annotation model from multiple scRNA-seq datasets generated on different sequencing platforms and you need batch-effect-free, pre-trained weights for downstream label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-reference-pretraining))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-signal-filtering**: Use when scRNA-seq count data contain a nuisance or confounding signal (cell cycle, sex, spatial zonation, batch, healthy reference) that must be removed at the gene-expression level while preserving the biological attribute of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-signal-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-similarity-learning**: Use when you have a noisy single-cell RNA-seq expression matrix (with dropouts and outliers) and need a cell-to-cell similarity/distance metric for de novo clustering, dimension reduction, or 2-D/3-D visualization of heterogeneous populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-similarity-learning))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-simulation**: Use when you need controlled synthetic scRNA-seq data with known cell types, patients, and true condition distances to benchmark differential-state methods or test scalability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-simulation--3748f19230409f07))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking cell-cycle correction or latent-factor removal methods on synthetic scRNA-Seq data, or when testing how sparse an effect (fraction of control genes influenced) must be before a PCA-based detector like ccRemover misses it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-simulation--0e00da0a05140942))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-subclustering**: Use when a broad Seurat cluster is suspected to contain rare subpopulations (e.g. low median silhouette score, known rare cell types like CAF subtypes absent from original annotations) and standard Louvain clustering failed to resolve them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-subclustering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-synthetic-data-generation**: Use when a small pilot scRNA-seq count matrix is available and larger synthetic data of the original study size is needed to plan downstream analyses such as clustering or cell-type abundance estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-synthetic-data-generation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-topic-modeling**: Use when the input is a cell-by-gene scRNA-seq count matrix and the goal is to decompose expression into interpretable topics corresponding to latent biological functions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-topic-modeling))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-validation-splitting**: Use when tuning the scSHARP GCNModel (batch size, neighbor count k, EdgeConv layer count l, embedding size e) on scRNA-seq data with upstream consensus confident labels, and you need held-out cells to score candidate configurations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-validation-splitting))
- **single-cell-rsec-clustering**: Use when single-cell count data must be partitioned into cell-type/identity clusters that will be supplied as labels to a trajectory-inference method such as Slingshot, especially after projecting cells into a ZINB-WaVE low-dimensional space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rsec-clustering))
- **single-cell-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when a single-cell trajectory/dynamics method offers a pre-processing aggregation option for large datasets and you must quantify runtime speedup while verifying that downstream dynamical quantities (landscape, transition probabilities, path likelihoods) are preserved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-runtime-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-runtime-memory-benchmarking**: Use when comparing runtime, memory usage, and clustering performance (ARI) of scRNA-seq integration/clustering methods across increasing dataset sizes, or quantifying device speed-up (CPU vs GPU) of a deep-learning single-cell model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-runtime-memory-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-sample-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when multiple sample-level single-cell/pathomics methods must be compared on patient clustering and trajectory accuracy across many datasets with uniform metrics and statistical ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-sample-clustering-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-sample-level-prior-modeling**: Use when you have multi-sample single-cell count data with sample-level metadata (e.g., disease status) and need a latent embedding whose sample-specific transformations are modeled as a function of condition, enabling downstream vector field and TF activity analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-sample-level-prior-modeling))
- **single-cell-sample-metadata-annotation**: Use when scRNA-seq samples already have cell-type annotations and you must attach per-sample quantitative metadata (cell-type percentages, composite niche scores, biological labels) for downstream group testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-sample-metadata-annotation))
- **single-cell-scatac-analysis**: Single-cell ATAC-seq analysis with Signac (R/Seurat) and ArchR. Process 10X Genomics scATAC data, perform QC, dimensionality reduction, clustering, peak calling, and motif activity scoring with chromVAR. Use when analyzing single-cell ATAC-seq data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/scatac-analysis) · ★1)
- **single-cell-scngs-read-extraction**: Use when constructing simulated single-cell scWGS benchmark data that must preserve allele-specific haplotype structure, controlled phase-switch error rates, and real WGA artifacts — e.g., before evaluating BAF/phasing-based CNA callers on ground-truth events spanning 200 kb to 5 Mb. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-scngs-read-extraction))
- **single-cell-screen-qc**: Qualify a single-cell Perturb-seq screen's no-phenotype calls with karyon — flag perturbations called "no effect" where the guide never actually knocked the target down (a silent failure), so the null is an artifact, not biology. Use for Perturb-seq QC, single-cell CRISPR screen silent-failure / false-negative qualification, on-target knockdown reliability, low-cell-count power checks, or deciding whether a "no-phenotype" perturbation is a true negative or just a failed knockdown. ([Curtisflo/karyon](https://github.com/Curtisflo/karyon/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-screen-qc))
- **single-cell-scrna-seq-analysis-scanpy**: Complete single-cell RNA-seq analysis workflow built on Scanpy and AnnData. Use this skill when: (1) Loading diverse single-cell data formats (10X, h5ad, CSV), (2) Performing quality control and filtering, (3) Normalization, dimensionality reduction, and clustering, (4) Marker gene identification and cell type annotation. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-scrna-seq-analysis-scanpy) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-scrnaseq-synthetic-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a cell-state decomposition or clustering method recovers known planted identity and activity patterns from synthetic scRNA-seq data with planted ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-scrnaseq-synthetic-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-secretion**: Pointer for analyzing microfluidic single-cell cytokine secretion data (IsoPlexis / IsoLight / IsoSpark and similar platforms). TRIGGER when the user mentions IsoPlexis, IsoLight, IsoSpark, IsoSpeak, IsoCode, CodePlex, polyfunctionality, PSI, Polyfunctional Strength Index, single-cell cytokine secretion, multiplex secretion CSV, or per-cell cytokine intensities. ([miller-jensen-lab/claude-code-marketplace](https://github.com/miller-jensen-lab/claude-code-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/mjlab/skills/single-cell-secretion))
- **single-cell-senescence-signature-scoring**: Use when scoring single-cell transcriptomes (AnnData, normalized but unscaled) for cellular senescence with cell-type-specific signatures, e.g., quantifying senescent-cell burden across ages or disease conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-senescence-signature-scoring))
- **single-cell-seurat-object-processing**: Use when starting from raw or unprocessed scRNA-seq data — Seurat objects, .rds count matrices, or paired count/metadata files — that must be loaded, normalized, and clustered before cell-type annotation with the scMiko/Miko scoring pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-seurat-object-processing))
- **single-cell-signalling-entropy-estimation**: Use when scRNA-Seq expression matrices need potency ordering of single cells (e.g. pluripotent vs multipotent vs differentiated, developmental stage ordering, cancer-vs-microenvironment contrast) without prior marker or timepoint knowledge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-signalling-entropy-estimation))
- **single-cell-signature-annotation**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data (AnnData object) must be scored or annotated for cellular senescence per cell type, or when literature-curated in vivo senescence gene signatures (e.g. Lung-fibroblast hubs) need to be extracted, searched, or merged. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-signature-annotation))
- **single-cell-similarity-matrix-transformation**: Use when a raw gene/protein expression CSV matrix (e.g. prot_expression.csv) plus class labels (target.csv) must be transformed before building the GraphDDP/GraphEmbed similarity graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-similarity-matrix-transformation))
- **single-cell-simulation-anchored-to-reference-dataset**: Use when benchmarking differential state (DS) methods requires simulated multi-sample scRNA-seq data that faithfully mimic a reference dataset's characteristics (mean-dropout, mean-variance, sample-to-sample variability). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-simulation-anchored-to-reference-dataset))
- **single-cell-simulation-api**: Guide for building ME (morpho-electric) models and running single-neuron simulations via the OBI REST APIs. Covers ME model creation (morphology + e-model compatibility check + registration), e-feature extraction, simulation campaigns, and real-time simulation via the small-scale-simulator. Also covers using bluecellulab in the sandbox as a workaround when task/launch has auth issues. ([openbraininstitute/obi-skills](https://github.com/openbraininstitute/obi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-simulation-api))
- **single-cell-simulation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing annotation accuracy of marker-database-based cell-type annotation methods on data with known ground-truth labels (e.g. a simulated PBMC dataset), or when deciding which marker database (CellMarker, PanglaoDB, Clustermole) to trust for easyct-based annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-simulation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **single-cell-simulation-benchmarking**: Use when a computational method for trajectory inference, trajectory alignment, RNA velocity, or cell-specific gene regulatory network inference (CSNI) must be evaluated quantitatively against fully known ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-simulation-benchmarking--47d9c95f7c45d50d))
- **single-cell-simulation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing interaction-detection performance of a new trajectory method against DE-based alternatives on simulated single-cell datasets with known ground-truth interaction labels, as in Fig 1f. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-simulation-benchmarking--e85e808ff07645b7))
- **single-cell-simulation-scenario-benchmarking**: Use when you need to rank or compare scRNA-seq/SRT simulation methods by functionality — e.g., which simulator reproduces cell groups, DEGs, batches, or differentiation trajectories — and want reproducible scores for each method per scenario. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-simulation-scenario-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-single-nucleotide-variant-calling**: Use when you need per-cell SNV or RNA-editing quantification from full-length Nanopore scRNA-seq data, e.g. computing edited vs total UMI molecule counts per cell cluster for a known SNP position. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-single-nucleotide-variant-calling))
- **single-cell-sketch-sampling**: Use when a scRNA-seq expression matrix is too large for downstream tools (clustering, trajectory inference) and an evenly covering cell subsample is needed, e.g. hundreds of thousands to millions of cells in CSV form. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-sketch-sampling))
- **single-cell-snn-graph-construction**: Use when a QC-filtered, normalized, scaled scRNA-seq object with variable features and PCA coordinates must be converted into a cell-to-cell SNN graph to enable graph-based (Louvain) clustering and downstream marker identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-snn-graph-construction))
- **single-cell-snp-genotyping**: Use when you have a 10x possorted_genome BAM plus barcodes.tsv from a multi-person cell mixture and need per-cell SNP genotypes to genetically separate contributors into individual-specific clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-snp-genotyping))
- **single-cell-snv-calling**: Use when starting from raw scRNA-seq reads (GEO SRA/FASTQ) and you need per-cell SNV calls and gene count matrices as SSrGE inputs, e.g. to build eeSNV features for cancer subpopulation identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-snv-calling--5d2cca6471509ec8))
- **single-cell-snv-calling**: Use when a 10x Genomics cell-barcoded tumor scRNA-seq sample (e.g., AML patient) must be split into malignant vs non-malignant populations directly from expressed somatic variants rather than from expression clustering alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-snv-calling--f72494b5793bb1a3))
- **single-cell-snv-rate-assignment**: Use when single cells come from a mixing experiment between lines with known genetic differences (e.g. BJ vs HEK293T) and each cell's line identity must be established from SNV support rates rather than barcodes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-snv-rate-assignment))
- **single-cell-snv-read-count-likelihood-modeling**: Use when joint inference of a tumor clonal tree is needed from SNV read counts where bulk DNA WES alternative/total matrices must be combined with scRNA-seq counts whose zeros arise from bursting, dropouts, or non-cancerous cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-snv-read-count-likelihood-modeling))
- **single-cell-spatial**: Activate FIRST for scRNA/h5ad, QC, markers, integration, lineage and spatial analysis; scATAC/Multiome. Then READ its selection guide, OPEN the chosen method's SKILL.md, and FOLLOW that method's documented steps/scripts before writing any analysis or Bash. ([canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang](https://github.com/canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang/tree/HEAD/plugins/market/Knowledge/bio-research-guided/skills/single-cell-spatial))
- **single-cell-spatial-cell-type-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how well a spatial transcriptomics integration method identifies cell types or decomposes spot-level gene expression on simulated benchmarks with ground-truth single-cell assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spatial-cell-type-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-spatial-coordinate-mapping**: Use when you have a paired single-cell RNA-seq matrix and ST spot-level count matrix with spot coordinates (e.g. Visium, SeqFISH-derived ST) and need to assign each single cell pseudo-spatial coordinates or infer SC-SC spatial adjacency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spatial-coordinate-mapping))
- **single-cell-spatial-dissimilarity-matrix-construction**: Use when you have an scRNA-seq matrix (cells x genes) plus a spatial dataset with coordinates for a small set of measured genes, and need to build cost/distance matrices before solving a spatial optimal transport mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spatial-dissimilarity-matrix-construction))
- **single-cell-spatial-location-reconstruction**: Use when a query SC dataset (e.g., .h5ad) lacks coordinates but a matched ST reference (SeqFISH/Visium-like) with truth x/y locations and overlapping genes is available, and spatially-aware downstream analyses such as co-localization or CCC are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spatial-location-reconstruction))
- **single-cell-spatial-map-visualization**: Use when per-cell intensities and centroid coordinates are already extracted (notebook 04) and the goal is to visualize local metabolite competition or expression as a spatial map per imaging region relative to landmarks like CD31+ vessels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-spatial-map-visualization))
- **single-cell-spatial-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when you have raw IMC and SIMS image data from the same tissue region(s) and need to: (1) register the two modalities spatially, (2) segment individual cells across both images, (3) extract per-cell protein and metabolite intensity vectors, and (4) prepare the data for downstream joint analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spatial-metabolomics-data-processing))
- **single-cell-spatial-reconstruction**: Use when scRNA-seq data must be mapped onto 2D/3D tissue coordinates without marker genes or a reference atlas, e.g. BDTNP dge.txt/geometry.txt pairs where spatial fidelity should be validated against FISH. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spatial-reconstruction))
- **single-cell-spliced-expression-aggregation**: Use when noisy per-cell scVelo RNA velocity needs smoothing before trajectory graph construction, i.e., an AnnData object with a spliced count matrix and velocity is available and meta-cell-level outputs are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-spliced-expression-aggregation))
- **single-cell-splicing**: Analyzes alternative splicing at single-cell resolution. The first decision is library chemistry — 10X 3' is fundamentally limited (RT primes from poly-A, R2 falls in 3' UTR, &lt;0.1 junction read per cell per AS event). Plate-based full-length methods (Smart-seq3, FLASH-seq, VASA-seq, STORM-seq) and single-cell long-read (MAS-Iso-seq, scISOr-Seq2) are the chemistries that give per-cell isoform structure. Tools include MARVEL (R, Smart-seq integrated), BRIE2 (Bayesian PSI with regulatory features and ELBO_gain test), scQuint (junction-cluster, plate-based; not for 10X), SpliZ (annotation-free Z-… ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/alternative-splicing-single-cell-splicing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-splicing**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Transcriptomics/alternative-splicing/single-cell-splicing) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **single-cell-splicing-landscape-clustering**: Use when single-cell SMART-seq (bam or junction-file) data must be partitioned into cell subpopulations where gene-expression-based clustering fails, e.g. TNBC tumor cells, embryonic hepatoblasts, or heterogeneous tumor-associated CD4/CD8 T cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-splicing-landscape-clustering))
- **single-cell-splicing-psi-quantification**: Use when single-cell (e.g., Cell Ranger-aligned FASTQ to hg19) exon-exon junction reads are available and the goal is to resolve modality-specific splicing-defined subpopulations not detectable from gene expression alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-splicing-psi-quantification))
- **single-cell-sql-query-execution**: Use when an AnnData/.h5ad single-cell dataset has been converted into an AnnSQL DuckDB database and you need to run SELECT-style SQL (including joins on layer tables) and capture results as a DataFrame, AnnData object, or Parquet file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-sql-query-execution))
- **single-cell-state-binarization**: Use when scRNA-seq data (log2(TPM+1)-style, cells as columns) must be converted into Boolean states for simulating a prior-knowledge signaling network and mapping each sequenced cell to a characteristic pathway attractor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-state-binarization))
- **single-cell-stratified-subsampling**: Use when you need to reduce a large scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. the 89,429-cell mTC mouse T cell .h5ad) to a per-cell-type sketch, and you must choose or reproduce a DV-guided within-type selection strategy (FB, MTB, or TP) given the number of cell types present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-stratified-subsampling))
- **single-cell-study-integration-metadata-harmonization**: Use when multiple single-cell RNA/ATAC/CITE-seq studies in H5AD format must be imported into a common queryable database and a gene's specific expression must be compared across studies to identify the annotated cell types it marks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-study-integration-metadata-harmonization))
- **single-cell-subclustering-louvain**: Use when a coarse cell-type annotation (e.g., 'dermal condensate') must be resolved into finer transcriptional states, and downstream tools like CellChat require state-resolved cell groups as input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-subclustering-louvain))
- **single-cell-subpopulation-clustering**: Use when scRNA-seq cells need to be grouped into subpopulations using either SNV-based features (eeSNVs from SSrGE) or raw gene-expression matrices, and when cluster quality must be benchmarked against known labels with AMI over bootstrap runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-subpopulation-clustering))
- **single-cell-subpopulation-selection**: Use when a processed scRNA-seq expression matrix with cell-level metadata (cell type, patient outcome) must be narrowed to one cell type before co-expression network inference, e.g. isolating classical monocytes from PBMCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-subpopulation-selection))
- **single-cell-subsampling-method-benchmarking**: Use when comparing sketching/subsampling methods on large scRNA-seq datasets (tens of thousands to millions of cells) with metrics like runtime, Gini coefficient, Hausdorff distance, or downstream deconvolution/annotation accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-subsampling-method-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-subtype-annotation-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking deep-learning cell annotation on tumor microenvironment scRNA-seq data, specifically to test whether major-type prediction followed by Seurat subclustering improves T/NK or myeloid subtype accuracy over direct per-cell annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-subtype-annotation-evaluation))
- **single-cell-supercell-construction**: Use when building a GRN from large single-cell transcriptomics data (e.g. snRNA-seq count matrices from a specific cell type like microglia or whole visium data) where full-transcriptome GRN inference is computationally intractable and per-cell sparsity must be reduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-supercell-construction))
- **single-cell-synthetic-spectra-handling**: Use when a continuous positive separation/threshold parameter (Delta) must be tuned so that same-label samples stay closer than different-label samples in a reduced marker subspace, with accuracy on a held-out test split as the objective. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-synthetic-spectra-handling))
- **single-cell-te-expression-quantification**: Use when you have 10x-style scRNA-seq BAM files (Cell Ranger CB:Z/UB:Z or STARsolo CR:Z/UR:Z tags) and need per-cell TE expression counts to study TE heterogeneity during development, reprogramming, or differentiation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-te-expression-quantification))
- **single-cell-te-quantification**: Use when single-cell short-read data (10X scRNA, Smart-seq2 scRNA, scATAC-seq, Visium, multi-omics) requires TE quantification where multi-mapping reads from repetitive TEs must be accurately assigned to specific loci rather than collapsed to subfamily level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-te-quantification))
- **single-cell-temporal-binning-factor-profiling**: Use when multi-batch single-cell data has a continuous per-cell time axis (e.g. scVelo latent time) and you need to test whether learned interpretable factors (JOINTLY modules) show stage-specific or monotonic temporal activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-temporal-binning-factor-profiling))
- **single-cell-tld-hierarchical-decoding**: Use when input is sparse single-cell Hi-C contact matrices (e.g., Lee human prefrontal cortex cells) and the goal is to detect hierarchical TAD-like domains per cell and test whether nested TLD features distinguish cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-tld-hierarchical-decoding))
- **single-cell-to-bulk-aggregation**: Use when raw single-cell GEO datasets (e.g. GSE81861 CRC, GSE72056 melanoma, GSE86146 embryonic) must be converted into bulk-aggregated expression matrices to validate cell-abundance inference or run bulk deconvolution baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-to-bulk-aggregation))
- **single-cell-to-bulk-decomposition**: Use when you have bulk RNA-seq expression counts from a large cohort plus snRNA-seq/scRNA-seq data from a subset of the same tissue (e.g., DLPFC), and need estimated cell-type proportions for phenotype association testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-to-bulk-decomposition))
- **single-cell-to-bulk-transfer-learning**: Use when you have a Miscell encoder trained on single-cell RNA-seq and a bulk tumor expression cohort (e.g., Mariathasan ICB or TCGA pan-cancer) whose clinical outcomes you want to link to latent features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-to-bulk-transfer-learning))
- **single-cell-to-cell-type-aggregation-nonzero-fraction**: Use when raw single-cell expression data must be collapsed to cell-type-level sender/receiver scores per ligand-receptor pair before assembling communication tensors across multiple samples or contexts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-to-cell-type-aggregation-nonzero-fraction))
- **single-cell-to-spot-mapping-matrix-inference**: Use when spot-level spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., MERFISH) must be deconvolved to single-cell resolution by mapping each SC profile onto ST spots, after both datasets share a trained embedding space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-to-spot-mapping-matrix-inference))
- **single-cell-topic-model-training**: Use when you need to integrate and cluster large-scale scRNA-seq datasets while obtaining interpretable topic-pathway associations from a cells-by-genes AnnData matrix with batch annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-topic-model-training))
- **single-cell-training-dynamics-monitoring**: Use when annotated scRNA-seq/spatial omics data (AnnData with a 'label' obs column) needs auditing for erroneous annotations, ambiguous intermediate states, or gene-trainability ranking via DNN training dynamics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-training-dynamics-monitoring))
- **single-cell-training-dynamics-scoring**: Use when an AnnData object carries an observation-column cell-type or condition label and you need per-cell goodness-of-match scores to detect mislabeled cells, ambiguous states, or label-difficulty patterns before downstream clustering or reannotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-training-dynamics-scoring))
- **single-cell-trajectory-backbone-construction**: Use when you have a clustering of single cells (from CLARA k-medoids) plus a low-dimensional cell embedding and need an acyclic tree-shaped trajectory topology connecting the clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-backbone-construction))
- **single-cell-trajectory-cell-cell-communication-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq data span a developmental time course without definitive cell types (e.g., early embryo pre-blastocyst stages), and the goal is to find development-related ligand-receptor pairs whose expression co-varies or causally regulates TF activity along pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-cell-cell-communication-analysis))
- **single-cell-trajectory-data-visualization**: Use when a pre-processed scRNA-seq anndata object with 'clusters' annotations and an scVelo RNA velocity field must be turned into a directed differentiation trajectory with per-population fate probabilities and terminal-state visualizations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-data-visualization))
- **single-cell-trajectory-embedding-visualization**: Use when you need a low-dimensional (d=2 or 3) manifold embedding and plot of single cells for trajectory visualization, starting from a preprocessed N cells × D genes expression matrix and the DTFLOW API. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-embedding-visualization))
- **single-cell-trajectory-fate-probability-estimation**: Use when time-series scRNA-seq data with time-point labels and cell type annotations is available and the goal is to predict cell fate bias — the probability a cell enters a particular fate given its initial state — including under simulated perturbations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-fate-probability-estimation))
- **single-cell-trajectory-fate-selection-testing**: Use when a slingshot SingleCellExperiment/trajectory object exists with per-cell lineage curve weights across ≥2 conditions and the goal is to test whether condition shifts the proportions of cells committed to each lineage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-fate-selection-testing))
- **single-cell-trajectory-file-formatting**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has per-cell RNA velocity vectors (spliced/unspliced-derived) that must be exported alongside 3D DR coordinates so CellexalVR can visualize trajectories as animated arrows/particles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-file-formatting))
- **single-cell-trajectory-gradient-visualization**: Use when you need to quantify how an in silico TF perturbation (e.g., Gata1 KO) shifts simulated cell-state transitions relative to the reference differentiation gradient in a scRNA-seq trajectory dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-gradient-visualization))
- **single-cell-trajectory-inference**: Infer developmental trajectories and pseudotime from single-cell RNA-seq data using Monocle3, Slingshot, and scVelo for RNA velocity analysis. Use when inferring developmental trajectories or pseudotime. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/trajectory-inference) · ★1)
- **single-cell-trajectory-inference**: Use when single-cell transcriptomic data from two or more conditions must be compared along a dynamic process (e.g., differentiation, EMT), and you need to decide whether a common trajectory suffices or conditions differ in topology, pseudotime progression, fate selection, or gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-inference--7b595acb83a745da))
- **single-cell-trajectory-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq data from a differentiating progenitor population must be ordered into a multi-branching trajectory with landmark states, without a priori temporal ordering of subsets, to detect branching fate decisions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-inference--c0922cd4af614073))
- **single-cell-trajectory-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq (or CyTOF/scATAC/imaging) data must be ordered along pseudotime and cell fates/terminal states auto-detected — e.g. rare delta cells in E15.5 pancreatic endocrinogenesis or dendritic subtypes in CD34+ hematopoiesis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-inference--a619ac8beb65c019))
- **single-cell-trajectory-inference-with-flow-networks**: Use when snapshot single-cell RNA-seq data spans multiple cell states (e.g., HSC-to-neutrophil/erythroid differentiation) and you need multi-state, transition-centered trajectories between any two chosen subpopulations without knowing branch counts a priori. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-inference-with-flow-networks))
- **single-cell-trajectory-mapping**: Use when new or perturbed cells (e.g. Gfi1-/-, Irf8-/-, double-KO GMP scRNA-seq count matrices) must be assigned to branches of an existing wild-type reference trajectory to quantify fate-biased allocation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-mapping))
- **single-cell-trajectory-method-comparison**: Use when comparing RNA-velocity-based trajectory inference (Cytopath) against topology-constrained baselines on a scRNA-seq dataset with pre-computed velocity and known root/terminal cell states and a known cell type ordering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-method-comparison))
- **single-cell-trajectory-reconstruction**: Use when scRNA-seq data span multiple stable and transitional cell states and the goal is to reconstruct trajectories between any two chosen subpopulations (e.g., HSPC-to-erythroid/GMP, B-cell/mono-DC-to-HSPC reprogramming) that linear or single-branch tools cannot resolve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-reconstruction))
- **single-cell-trajectory-simulation**: Use when you have longitudinal scRNA-seq data (normalized expression, time-point labels, cell type annotations, e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-simulation--cc22319afa3725e5))
- **single-cell-trajectory-simulation**: Use when benchmarking trajectory inference or pseudotime methods (e.g., CellPath, Slingshot, VeTra, CellRank) against a known ground truth that real scRNA-seq data cannot provide. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-simulation--cbacfc2c122398d1))
- **single-cell-trajectory-tree-alignment**: Use when two scRNA-seq datasets each contain branching pseudotime trajectories and you need to globally compare their cell-state transition trees, or benchmark how topological/clustering changes affect alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-tree-alignment))
- **single-cell-trajectory-tree-inference**: Use when you have a multi-sample scRNA-seq dataset with per-cell sample annotation and a low-dimensional embedding (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-tree-inference))
- **single-cell-trajectory-visualization**: Use when a latent space model (LatentVelo) has produced spliced_velocity and latent trajectories and you need to visualize dynamics — e.g., streamlines colored by latent_time, diverging trajectories toward multiple fates, or terminal-state comparisons on a UMAP embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-visualization--628506e2284fcb5e))
- **single-cell-trajectory-visualization**: Use when an AnnData object with spliced/unspliced counts has been fitted with UniTVelo (or scVelo) and you must render the velocity stream, latent time, phase portraits, or direction-correctness metrics to judge the trajectory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trajectory-visualization--537347ff70631ea2))
- **single-cell-transcript-reconstruction-with-isoquant**: Use when starting from barcoded, long-read (ONT or PacBio) alignments to a reference genome and needing known/novel transcript models plus per-droplet isoform and gene count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-transcript-reconstruction-with-isoquant))
- **single-cell-transcriptional-velocity-estimation**: Use when you have scRNA-seq expression data (e.g. GSE207943: 5501 cells x 15497 genes) plus a gene-gene regulatory mask or coexpression prior and Phenograph cluster labels, and need per-cell velocity vectors and cluster-specific regulatory interaction matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transcriptional-velocity-estimation))
- **single-cell-transcriptome-dynamics-modeling**: Use when the goal is to infer attractor basins, cell-fate transition paths, and transition cells from single-cell transcriptome data by modeling cell-state dynamics as a Markov random walk across cell-cell and cluster-cluster scales. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transcriptome-dynamics-modeling))
- **single-cell-transcriptome-projection**: Use when you need cell expression profiles and Cell Ontology cell-type terms to share one embedding space, e.g. to classify cells to unseen ontology terms or compute marker genes per ontology category. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transcriptome-projection))
- **single-cell-transcriptome-proteome-integration-workflow**: Use when paired single-cell transcriptome and protein count matrices with reference cell labels need a joint embedding, a clustering resolution sweep, and a report of agreement with the supplied labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/workflows/single-cell-transcriptome-proteome-integration))
- **single-cell-transcriptomic-sketching**: Use when a single-cell expression matrix (rows = cells, columns = genes/PCs) is too large for downstream clustering, trajectory inference, or visualization, and a fair, size-controlled cell subset that evenly covers the transcriptomic space is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transcriptomic-sketching))
- **single-cell-transcriptomics-data-handling**: Use when input consists of dyngen-simulated single-cell transcriptomics profiles (e.g. the 42 dyngen datasets from 14 backbones × 3 seeds) with paired cell-specific ground-truth GRNs, for network inference benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transcriptomics-data-handling))
- **single-cell-transcriptomics-predictive-modeling**: Use when you have a trained multi-omics predictor's ranked top features and need to check whether those features show elevated feature importance in an independent dataset (bulk NCI60 or single-cell transcriptomics) beyond chance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-transcriptomics-predictive-modeling))
- **single-cell-transfer-learning**: Use when a trained single-cell embedding model must be applied zero-shot to a new tissue or species AnnData (cells-by-genes .h5ad with 'batch_indices' and 'cell_types' obs columns) and clustering quality on the target must be quantified against ground-truth cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transfer-learning))
- **single-cell-transition-regulator-identification**: Use when single-cell trajectory data (e.g., CellRouter multi-state trajectories) and a CLR/CellNet-style GRN edge list are available and the goal is to identify transcriptional regulators driving or mediating specific cell-fate transitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transition-regulator-identification))
- **single-cell-transposable-element-quantification**: Use when single-cell scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq, multi-omics, Visium, or long-read data need locus-specific TE count matrices, especially when multi-mapping reads from repetitive TE sequences must be resolved beyond subfamily-level or random/-map assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-transposable-element-quantification))
- **single-cell-trs-background-correction**: Use when scoring individual cells for GWAS trait association from a Seurat scRNA-seq object — i.e., after trait-relevant genes (top 1,000 by Pearson correlation with gPASsum) are prioritized and per-cell p-values are needed (scPagwas singlecell = TRUE). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trs-background-correction))
- **single-cell-trs-score-computation**: Use when a ground-truth scRNA-seq dataset with cell-type labels and pruned GWAS summary statistics (e.g. monocyte count GWAS) must be scored per cell to measure how precisely trait-relevant cells are retrieved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-trs-score-computation))
- **single-cell-tsne-embedding**: Use when you have a chromVARDeviations object (per-cell motif deviation scores) from single-cell ATAC-seq data and need a 2D embedding/visualization of cell-to-cell similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-tsne-embedding))
- **single-cell-umap-annotation-visualization**: Use when an integrated scRNA-seq object (e.g. Human_HSPC normalized UMI matrix) needs cell-type annotation via AUCell enrichment of lineage signature GMT sets, with results rendered as a UMAP colored by assigned lineage labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umap-annotation-visualization))
- **single-cell-umap-clustering**: Use when you have a per-cell TE count matrix (from scTE or scTEATAC on scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq BAMs) and need to verify that cells cluster by known identity using TE features alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umap-clustering))
- **single-cell-umap-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when running gExcite's CITE-seq/ADT analysis layer on paired GEX+ADT (e.g. 10x hashed PBMC) data and you need cell-level similarity embeddings from one or both modalities, or joint BremSC clustering inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umap-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-umap-embedding-evaluation**: Use when comparing whether an alternative single-cell feature space (e.g., uTAR or aTAR expression) clusters cell types comparably to annotated gene expression, via silhouette coefficients on 2D UMAP embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umap-embedding-evaluation))
- **single-cell-umap-visualization**: Use when a trained single-cell multi-omics embedding (e.g., Mowgli latent dimensions on TEA-seq, PBMC 10X, OP Multiome, OP CITE, or BM CITE data) needs 2D visualization to inspect cell-type separation, compare methods, or overlay factor weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umap-visualization))
- **single-cell-umi-count-matrix-filtering**: Use when the input is a raw (unfiltered, unnormalized) gene-by-cell UMI count matrix, e.g. a Cell Ranger raw digital gene expression matrix, that must be cleaned and normalized before trajectory or downstream scRNA-seq analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umi-count-matrix-filtering))
- **single-cell-umi-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when simulating synthetic scRNA-seq UMI count matrices from real gene-by-cell count matrices (e.g., produced by Utility.scRNA_bam2countmat with UMI_modeling=True) to create a deduplication benchmark ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umi-count-matrix-simulation))
- **single-cell-umi-count-modeling**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI count matrices (e.g., PBMC4k 4182 cells × 1000 highly variable genes) must be modeled with per-gene negative-binomial dispersion, specifically to estimate or refresh the inverse dispersion parameter θ during deep generative model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umi-count-modeling))
- **single-cell-umi-count-normalization**: Use when raw UMI read count matrices from scRNA-seq (e.g., GSE120861 highmoi Perturb-seq) must be normalized at both mean and variance levels before linear association testing such as DE, CRISPR screen, or co-expression inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-umi-count-normalization))
- **single-cell-unspliced-rna-layer-processing**: Use when an scRNA-seq anndata contains spliced/unspliced count layers and downstream analysis (DeepCycle cell-cycle phase assignment, motif activity from unspliced counts) requires scVelo-computed moments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-unspliced-rna-layer-processing))
- **single-cell-unsupervised-clustering**: Use when clustering unlabeled single-cell transcriptomes (e.g. small organ tissue samples from the Tabula Sapiens atlas with manually annotated phenotypes) and you want to capture high-order gene coexpression interactions rather than rely on PCA/scVI reductions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-unsupervised-clustering))
- **single-cell-unwanted-variation-filtering**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data contain nuisance biological signals such as cell cycle or sex effects that must be removed before downstream analysis while preserving biological signal (e.g., genes of interest in corrected HVGs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-unwanted-variation-filtering))
- **single-cell-upstream**: 单细胞转录组上游流程：10x 及其他格式读取、QC 阈值、双细胞识别、归一化、HVG、PCA、批次整合选型、聚类与注释。 ([ForrestGump618/Eve](https://github.com/ForrestGump618/Eve/tree/HEAD/omicslab/skills/single-cell-upstream))
- **single-cell-utr-enrichment-annotation**: Use when you have aligned single-cell reads (BAM, e.g. 10x Chromium) plus a canonical GFF/GTF annotation and need novel or improved 3' UTR end calls, especially to benchmark against tools like GETUTR or UTRme. - **single-cell-variant-allele-frequency-inference**: Use when you have per-cell mutation VAF and coverage matrices from scRNA-seq variant calling that suffer from low coverage and allelic bias, and the goal is inferring corrected genotypes/VAFs or cell lineages. - **single-cell-variant-calling-with-molecular-barcodes**: Use when you have eWGS-discovered somatic SNVs and 10x Chromium BAMs from cells known to be wild-type (e.g., healthy donor bone marrow), and need to estimate per-site false-positive rates of scRNA-seq mutation calling. - **single-cell-variant-read-count-processing**: Use when scRNA-seq somatic SNV read counts (Rs, Xs) and bulk WES counts (Rb, Xb) must be subsetted to mutations with estimable bursting kinetics before running Canopy2 tree inference. - **single-cell-vdj-preprocessing**: Use when raw scVDJ-seq contigs (e.g. AIRR/contig formats) must be merged with a SingleCellExperiment GEX object and cells without complete, productive TCR information filtered out before pseudobulking or trajectory analysis.
